a much larger quantity. IF EVERY ADDED-VICTIM DOSE IS STILL ZERO AT BOTH SEEDS, THE DESIGN STOPS AND NO
CELLS ARE QUEUED. ***

## *** USER RULING: THE D250 PASS RULE IS REPLACED, BEFORE ANY BASELINE IS SCORED. ***

**Written here BEFORE the first D250 baseline lands, as the ruling requires.**
```
RUNG 3 PASSES D250 if BOTH hold:
```

**This replaces the earlier on-time-based rule and is consistent with D257 (3).**

*** ONE PROPERTY OF THE CHOSEN METRIC THAT MUST TRAVEL WITH EVERY SCORE, BECAUSE IT WAS MEASURED TODAY AND
IT CUTS BOTH WAYS: CORRECT-BY-DEADLINE INCLUDES DEVICE CREDIT. Per D155 (3) it counts the cloud's answer
inside the deadline, OTHERWISE THE DEVICE'S ANSWER IF THAT ALSO ARRIVES IN TIME. On the D250 gate cell that
is 49 cloud plus 23 DEVICE of 72, so a THIRD of the credit is device answers, and the device is 54 pct as
accurate as the cloud (0.371 against 0.681). Cloud-only correct-by-deadline is 49/132 = 0.371 against
82/132 = 0.621 for the reference, a 25 pp fall where the composite falls 10. ***
**So the pass rule is scored exactly as ruled, AND cloud-correct, device-standing and device-correct are
carried beside it so the reader can see both. Device credit in robot on-time remains an open user question
and this condition is where it decides rather than decorates.**

## *** HOST CONFLICT, 2026-11-25 11:31: THE GPU IS HELD AND STAYS HELD UNTIL THE USER RESOLVES IT. ***

**An outside actor's StudyREAL-NET processes trip the live-run preflight's exclusive-host check, so the
first filler cell was refused in 2 seconds and the driver set STOP plus watchdog HOLD.**
*** TWO RULINGS: (1) DO NOT clear STOP and HOLD when their processes go away. The preflight only checks AT
CELL START, so a cell that starts clean and has their workload launch at second 30 PASSES preflight and
produces CONTAMINATED numbers. A refused cell costs 2 seconds and is visible; a contaminated cell is a
number we would trust. Their run is 60 s against a 21-minute cell, so a restart into a gap would almost
certainly straddle the next launch. IDLE IS THE CORRECT STATE WHILE THE HOST IS CONTESTED. (2) DO NOT relax
or narrow the preflight. It refuses any host Python as a proxy for GPU 2 contention, which is STRICTER than
its name, and that strictness is a FEATURE. Narrowing it would let a future real contender through and
remove the guard that kept every committed cell clean. The answer to two workloads on one host is to move
one or serialise them, never to weaken the check that notices. ***
**P3 is CLEAN: it ended 11:30:51, nineteen seconds BEFORE their processes launched at ~11:31:10, so it does
not straddle the window.**

## *** USER RULING: THE DASHCAM-DEADLINE BUILD IS DEFERRED BEHIND THE D249 GATE CELL. THE ROBOT SWEEP PROCEEDS. ***

**(1) THE 0.5 s DASHCAM RESCORE IS HAPPENING NOW ON FLASH, CPU ONLY. Nothing for you.** It uses the
scorer's existing `L_dash` parameter (`score.py:752`), so it needs no code change and no cells, and it is
labelled *** "DELIVERY WITHIN 0.5 s, POLICY PLANNED FOR 1.0 s" *** because it answers WHO DELIVERED FAST,
not who would do well if the deadline were 0.5 s. Every arm optimised against the 1.0 s deadline it was
given.

## D238, USER RULING 2026-11-24, PROVISIONAL, revisit later (recorded first, before relaying): THE OVERNIGHT POLICY BLOCK, 28 CELLS

**Order, AFTER the three replicates now running (kv0.5r s4 `_rep2`, kv0.5r s4 `_rep3`, 1x s4 `_rep2`).**

**BLOCK A, the same-code 0.5x block: 9 cells, `kv0.5r_L3_epg_rq30`, seeds 5, 6, 7, INTERLEAVED BY SEED.**
Per seed, in this order: `covex-v6-fair-final` **`_rep2`**, then `covex-v6-fair-final-stateoff`, then
`covex-v6-fair-final-notouch`. So s5 three cells, then s6 three, then s7 three.
*Why interleaved by seed and not grouped by arm, since it matters if a cell fails:* interleaving puts each
seed's three arms adjacent in time, so a seed's comparison is internally same-code even if the tip moves
between seeds, and **a failure costs one seed rather than one arm across all seeds**. Grouping by arm would
make every comparison depend on the tip holding across the whole block.
*What block A is for:* **every ablation verdict on origin is seed 4 only, one cell per arm.** Three more
seeds with the comparator run beside the ablations, same code, is what turns three single-cell readings
into a per-seed distribution. It also supplies **same-seed comparator deltas**, which is the only way the
0.5x verdicts at 1.19x and 1.02x of the floor become decidable.
**If the per-type guard (`ptg`) cell is PROOF-READY, insert it DIRECTLY AFTER block A. If it is not,
SKIP IT AND REPORT; do not hold the queue for it.** Its bar 1 is the amended three-valued one
(`results/orchestration/live2/d231_ptg_guard/AMENDMENT_4_bar1_tolerance.md`): PASS, NO DIFFERENCE, or FAIL
against the max observed 0.027752, and **bars 2 and 3 do NOT inherit that floor.**

**BLOCK B, the 0.5x baseline sweep already queued: 8 cells.** `confidence-cascade` s6 and s7;
`covex-v6-base` s6 and s7; and s7 for `simplestream`, `llf`, `mooncake` and `eco-llm`.

**BLOCK C, the 0.5x ladder: 7 cells.** `covex-v6-rules` s5, s6, s7; `covex-v6-state-final` s4, s5, s6, s7.

**BLOCK D, `covex-v6-fair-final-notouch` at 1x (`L3_epg_rq30`): 4 cells, s4 to s7.**

**RULES.**
1. **NO PUSHES TO ANY FILE ON THE RUN PATH (`experiments/`) UNTIL BLOCK A FINISHES.** This binds flash,
   the Orin and every other session, not only the H100. **Record, for EVERY cell, the `experiments/` tree
   hash plus the blob hashes of `arms_final_ladder.py`, `arms_v6state.py` and `scorer/score.py`.** A pair
   counts as **same-code ONLY when all four match**; otherwise it is **stratum B and must be labelled so.**
   *This rule exists because it already cost us a floor today:* original versus `_rep2` is stratum B
   precisely because `arms_v6state.py` moved (the `-stateoff` and `-notouch` appends) and
   `arms_final_ladder.py` moved (ptg's `42aa4fb`). **And "same-code by construction" is not available as
   an argument: the driver pulls origin before every cell, so any session's push moves the tip. Verify by
   hash, never by construction.**
2. **Every cell needs a `source_agreement` verdict AND `metrics_health`.** **INVALID cells are EXCLUDED and
   rerun ONCE at the end of the block they belong to.** `run_meta.run_valid` is **TRUE** on torn cells, so
   it does not catch this class; read `header.source_agreement.verdict`.
3. **Gates as before. No 0.25x cells. Never seeds 1 to 3. Refuse any build or proof below 15 GB free. No
   code push inside a cell's start-up window. Never `pkill -f`. GPU 2 is exclusive.**
4. **SCORING: score each cell AS IT LANDS, but issue NO VERDICT on block A or block D until every seed in
   that block is in.** Then report **per seed AND pooled**, against the D234 floor and against the
   `_rep2`/`_rep3` pair. *The reason the verdict waits:* a per-seed reading published early becomes the
   number everyone quotes, and four attainment verdicts were already voided today for sitting inside a
   floor. **Pooling across seeds is also the only thing that can distinguish a real effect from the
   seed-level variation that one cell per arm cannot separate.**
5. **Git identity from the existing config only; no AI attribution; no force-push, reset, stash or amend.**

**REPORT: the queue with ETAs, and any INVALID, saturated or failed cell AS IT HAPPENS, not at the end.**

## Standing rules (unchanged)

- GPU 2 (H100 NVL, uuid GPU-4f1d7e47-...) is exclusive to our harness; never touch a process that is not ours.
- No host Python while a vLLM server is up, except the harness clients. No heavy jq/zcat near a run boundary: wait for
  the next run's `start` line plus 60 s.
- Never seeds 1 to 3, never HOLDOUT, never delete frames, never use or recompute from an INVALID cell, never rerun a
  valid cell.
- Stop a harness only with SIGINT (never SIGTERM: it orphans vLLM); then clear servers of ours whose parent is 1.
- D166: commit / push our own driver files, run results and this file until 2026-12-04. Single-line messages, NO
  Co-Authored-By or any Claude attribution. Fetch first, rebase only unpushed commits; never force-push, reset, stash or
  amend. No secrets (hf-token.txt), no frames, nothing over 5 MB.
- D106/D116: no methodological change; anything unforeseen is PROVISIONAL and logged. A blocker skips that item.
- Never start a cell across a push that changes the coordinator: the driver pulls only between cells, and each
  cell's `start` line in `results/orchestration/live2/frozen_test/frozen_grid.log` records the HEAD it ran on.

## Gating a posted list (the operator's check; nothing runs without it)

For a new file `runs/<LIST>` on origin:
1. `git pull --rebase` and confirm HEAD contains flash's posting commit (`git merge-base --is-ancestor <hash> HEAD`).
2. sha256 of the file matches the hash flash posted.
3. Proofs pass in or below that commit (read the named JSONs: verdicts PASS; off-switch identity against committed
   digests; port rule, i.e. the arm's characteristic decision occurs at least once; step 2b).
4. The arm name resolves through `run_live2_real` with a negative control (flash's name-proof JSON), the name is
   distinct from existing arms, and no `harm_warm` is declared (computed is fine).
5. Any code changed AFTER the proofs' commit must be read: decision-path changes block the gate; instrumentation-only
   changes may pass, PROVISIONAL, logged.
6. Then append `<LIST> <sha256>` to the LOCAL gitignored `runs/V6_GATE_OK` and log an `OPERATOR:` line in
   frozen_grid.log. If any check fails, do not gate; record why below.

- 23:55 correct_by_deadline PROXY CAVEAT, FOR THE BLOCK VERDICT (source: d231_ptg_prereg.json AMENDMENT_3_D232_CLOSES_BOTH_OPEN_FLAGS.flag_1_CLOSED_bar_2_is_readable.CAVEAT_THAT_MUST_BE_CARRIED_check_6_proxy_validity): the device half never binds, so under the D155 (3) rule (cloud if in time, else device, else none) a LATE cloud answer still scores 1 whenever the device answer is correct. The metric's sensitivity to cloud timeliness is attenuated by device accuracy (device_only_accuracy is 0.295238 at s7 over all questions; on the late-cloud subset the rate may differ: flash's 'right 0.295238 of the time' applies the pooled rate to that subset and is approximate). So the stateoff correct_by_deadline deltas (-0.118110 / -0.231405 / -0.114285) mix cloud timeliness with device accuracy, the device part carrying no timing information; an arm that falls back to a correct device answer scores the same as one delivering a correct cloud answer on time. This EXPLAINS the s6/s7 divergence between correct_by_deadline and robot cloud on-time: they measure different things (expected, not anomalous). Carried beside EVERY correct_by_deadline figure.
